He lives in a small house with Lan and her family. Every morning, Max wakes up early and runs to the garden. He loves to play with his red ball.</p>\n383\t                <p>One day, Max found a tiny kitten under the tree. The kitten was lost and scared. Max wagged his tail and sat next to the kitten. Soon, they became the best of friends.</p>\n384\t              </div>\n385\t            </div>\n386\t\n387\t            <div class=\"passage-rec-row\">\n388\t              <p class=\"passage-rec-label\">Record yourself reading the passage aloud. <span class=\"vi\">Ghi âm bạn đọc đoạn văn.</span></p>\n389\t              <div class=\"rec-slot\" id=\"rec-r3-junior\"></div>\n390\t            </div>\n391\t\n392\t            <div class=\"comp-questions\">\n393\t              <div class=\"comp-section-label\">Comprehension / Câu hỏi đọc hiểu</div>\n394\t\n395\t              <div class=\"comp-q\">\n396\t                <label class=\"comp-label\" for=\"r3j-q1\">Q1 — What colour is Max? <span class=\"vi\">(Màu của Max?)</span></label>\n397\t                <input type=\"text\" id=\"r3j-q1\" placeholder=\"Max is…\" />\n398\t              </div>\n399\t\n400\t              <div class=\"comp-q\">\n401\t                <label class=\"comp-label\" for=\"r3j-q2\">Q2 — What does Max love to do in the garden? <span class=\"vi\">(Max thích làm gì trong vườn?)</span></label>\n402\t                <input type=\"text\" id=\"r3j-q2\" placeholder=\"Max loves to…\" />\n403\t              </div>\n404\t\n405\t              <div class=\"comp-q\">\n406\t                <label class=\"comp-label\" for=\"r3j-q3\">Q3 — What did Max find under the tree? <span class=\"vi\">(Max tìm thấy gì dưới gốc cây?)</span></label>\n407\t                <input type=\"text\" id=\"r3j-q3\" placeholder=\"Max found…\" />\n408\t              </div>\n409\t\n410\t              <div class=\"comp-q\">\n411\t                <label class=\"comp-label\" for=\"r3j-q4\">Q4 — Do you have a pet? Young people who speak English with confidence earn more, travel more freely, and access opportunities that others cannot reach. But most students spend years studying grammar rules and still freeze the moment they need to speak.</p>\n426\t                <p>Why? Because schools teach English as a code to crack, not a voice to find. The students who break through are the ones who stopped waiting to be perfect and started speaking anyway. They made mistakes, got corrected, and kept going. That is the only path.</p>\n427\t              </div>\n428\t            </div>\n429\t\n430\t            <div class=\"passage-rec-row\">\n431\t              <p class=\"passage-rec-label\">Record yourself reading the passage aloud. <span class=\"vi\">Ghi âm bạn đọc đoạn văn.</span></p>\n432\t              <div class=\"rec-slot\" id=\"rec-r3-senior\"></div>\n433\t            </div>\n434\t\n435\t            <div class=\"comp-questions\">\n436\t              <div class=\"comp-section-label\">Comprehension / Câu hỏi đọc hiểu</div>\n437\t\n438\t              <div class=\"comp-q\">\n439\t                <label class=\"comp-label\" for=\"r3s-q1\">Q1 — Why do most students freeze when they need to speak? <span class=\"vi\">(Tại sao học sinh thường đứng hình khi cần nói?)</span></label>\n440\t                <textarea id=\"r3s-q1\" placeholder=\"Because…\"></textarea>\n441\t              </div>\n442\t\n443\t              <div class=\"comp-q\">\n444\t                <label class=\"comp-label\" for=\"r3s-q2\">Q2 — What do students who break through have in common? <span class=\"vi\">(Điểm chung của những học sinh tiến bộ?)</span></label>\n445\t                <textarea id=\"r3s-q2\" placeholder=\"They…\"></textarea>\n446\t              </div>\n447\t\n448\t              <div class=\"comp-q\">\n449\t                <label class=\"comp-label\" for=\"r3s-q3\">Q3 — What does \"a code to crack\" mean in your own words? <span class=\"vi\">(Cụm từ \"a code to crack\" nghĩa là gì theo cách hiểu của bạn?)</span></label>\n450\t                <textarea id=\"r3s-q3\" placeholder=\"I think it means…\"></textarea>\n451\t              </div>\n452\t\n453\t              <div class=\"comp-q\">\n454\t                <label class=\"comp-label\" for=\"r3s-q4\">Q4 — Do you agree that making mistakes is necessary? Why or why not? <span class=\"vi\">(Bạn có đồng ý rằng mắc lỗi là cần thiết không?)</span></label>\n455\t                <textarea id=\"r3s-q4\" placeholder=\"I agree / disagree because…\"></textarea>\n456\t              </div>\n457\t\n458\t            </div>\n459\t          </div><!-- /#r3-senior -->\n460\t\n461\t        </div>\n462\t      </section>\n463\t\n464\t      <!-- ROOM 4: SPEAKING -->\n465\t      <section class=\"room-panel\" id=\"room-4\" aria-label=\"Room 4 — Speaking\">\n466\t        <div class=\"card\">\n467\t          <div class=\"card-title\">Room 4 — Free Speaking</div>\n468\t          <p class=\"room-intro\">\n469\t            Pick a topic and just talk. No preparation, no script — this is your real voice, not your homework voice.\n470\t            <span class=\"vi\"> / Chọn chủ đề và nói tự nhiên. [...document.querySelectorAll(`input[name=\"${name}\"]:checked`)].map(el => el.value);\n    }\n    function val(id) { return document.getElementById(id)?.value.trim() ?? ''; }\n\n    function collectRoom1() {\n      const age = document.querySelector('.age-card.active')?.dataset.age;\n      if (age === 'junior') {\n        return {\n          branch: 'junior',\n
